## Corpus Check
- 2912 files · ~2,186,667 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 22023 nodes · 38475 edges · 1871 communities (1461 shown, 410 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 272 edges (avg confidence: 0.85)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `62787d62`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
